How to read it
  • Thesis: the earnings move will be bigger than the options imply (view = 1.5x the implied move). Every structure is net long volatility with risk capped at the debit.
  • Pick: the lowest net debit among structures that make money on both an up and a down move of 1.5x IM after the IV crush. P&L columns show the worse of up/down.
  • Implied move is the event-only expected move: 30d IV is split into base vol + one earnings jump worth E=12 normal trading days.
  • Run-up play: enter today, close at the bell before the release (spot flat and ±0.5 sd). Uses calendars/double calendars/financed strangles that sell the pre-earnings week to pay for the post-earnings expiry.
  • Fills: buy ask / sell bid, half-spread max(3%, $0.02) on entry and exit. Flat smile model unless a real chain is supplied (--chain).
  • Calendar-type picks (front leg sold before the release) are meant to be closed before the announcement or rolled into a pure long strangle after the front expires.
